# 2004 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** college network · Gmail invite
**Star:** thefacebook networks · `itt04-thefacebook-networks` · `sites/facebook/networks.html`
**ILS June:** 51,611,646 · 910,060,180
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 676
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt04-thefacebook-networks`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2004/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2004/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2004 — Web 2.0 hinge
2. thefacebook networks — college graph REAL
3. Firefox 1.0 — tabs · popups
4. Gmail — 1GB invite lore
5. Flickr · Thefacebook
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt04-thefacebook-networks`

**Room:** `years/2004/sites/facebook/networks.html`
**Hook:** `data-fb-join`
**Nostalgia:** thefacebook networks — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- 0 networks · Gmail-as-gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt04-thefacebook-networks`.

**Complete writes**
- Join a college network. Writes itt04-thefacebook-networks.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2004` · key `itt04-thefacebook-networks`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-thefacebook-networks` — do not write `itt03-*` or `itt05-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Friends / poke → `years/2004/sites/facebook/friends.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

### L-0001 · `itt04-adsense-rlx` · sites/adsense/index.html

**Open:** `/years/2004/sites/adsense/index.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="adsense-rlx"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “adsense leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `google` · `facebook`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="adsense-rlx"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt04-adsense-rlx']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt04-thefacebook-networks` stays empty. `itt03-adsense-rlx` and `itt05-adsense-rlx` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “adsense leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="adsense-rlx"]`.
- `localStorage['itt04-adsense-rlx']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2004", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt04-adsense-rlx`.
- Does **not** write `itt04-thefacebook-networks`.
- **Next:** altavista leftover → `/years/2004/sites/altavista/index.html`
